# SC18: SF4 stronger Fair CPU measuring report

2026-11-25. Version `1.5.29`. Ticket [#210](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/210).

382 of 480 new-versus-old cells meet both win and idle bars; 358 of 360 adjacent-level cells meet the higher-level win bar. 35 win bars miss under the all-attempts denominator, including 8 additional overall misses beyond the nominal bars.

**Readiness:** the stronger CPU remains off by default behind `--stronger-cpu`. Remaining measuring gates are tracked in [#275](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/275); [#270](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/270) covers untranslated-branch stops and [#274](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/274) the confirmed Adon native hang. The [SC24 enablement decision](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/216) and human playtest remain the maintainer's.

## Source and method

Every final measuring table uses source [944e92a](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/commit/944e92a6f0a39953b95a1b0d6c4dc47eff955be9) (`944e92a6f0a39953b95a1b0d6c4dc47eff955be9`), with recorded measuring executable SHA-256 `0a9ae1f52fd1ed5e4b159d1767a3f134a413fb0f7afdeb5f4266017c4ffd36ac`.

The grid contains 60 selectable fighter/revision/Ultra pairs: every tournament fighter in USF4 with Ultra I, Ultra II and Ultra Double; Ryu, Ken, Gen and Sagat also in SF4, SSF4 and AE2012; and Adon also in SSF4 and AE2012. Every pair has eight primary cells (four levels, both sides), six adjacent-level cells, and one old-versus-old control at Very Difficult.

There were **216,000 attempted matches, 209,706 completed matches, 6,293 engine-stopped matches and 1 unfinished matches**. Seeds 1-240 were accounted for exactly once in every cell; the final run used 8 worker threads.

| Group | Cells | Pass / miss | Attempted | Completed | Stopped | Unfinished | Stops-as-loss win misses |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| primary | 480 | 382 / 98 | 115200 | 111310 | 3889 | 1 | 33 |
| adjacent | 360 | 358 / 2 | 86400 | 85522 | 878 | 0 | 2 |
| old-old | 60 | not graded | 14400 | 12874 | 1526 | 0 | not graded |

New-versus-old requires at least half of recorded rows won and strictly less idle. Adjacent levels require the higher level to win more than half. Wins-losses-draws count only completed matches; unfinished rows are separate and prevent a nominal pass. Recorded-row win denominators still include unfinished rows and completed draws. The separate stops-as-loss win bar uses all 240 attempted seeds, including every stop and unfinished row, without relabeling either as a completed loss or draw. Idle and attack rates are weighted by all recorded live frames from per-match values rounded to two decimals; stopped matches have no rate and are excluded from those rate comparisons.

Levels 1-4 are Easy, Normal, Difficult and Very Difficult. In primary rows, first/second means new/old; in adjacent rows it means higher/lower; in old-versus-old rows it means P1/P2.

Equivalent grid commands reconstructed exactly from the manifests (they do not assert how resumptions were invoked):

```text
rtk proxy python target/sc18/measure_sc18.py final --binary "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\bin\cpu_measure_sc18-944e92a.exe" --out "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\final-944e92a" --source 944e92a6f0a39953b95a1b0d6c4dc47eff955be9 --count 240 --threads 8
rtk proxy python target/sc18/measure_sc18.py controls --binary "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\bin\cpu_measure_pre-sc18-9bded71.exe" --out "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\baseline-controls" --source 9bded71f0b870dcc09c2e71cc5a12ee0d52d507e --count 40 --threads 4
rtk proxy python target/sc18/measure_sc18.py controls --binary "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\bin\cpu_measure_sc18-944e92a.exe" --out "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\controls-944e92a" --source 944e92a6f0a39953b95a1b0d6c4dc47eff955be9 --count 40 --threads 8
```

Reproduce an individual table row with the same frozen executable, fighter/style, side flags, levels and seed range:

```text
rtk proxy "D:\sf1-port-storage\build\original-target\sc18\bin\cpu_measure_sc18-944e92a.exe" "<out.tsv>" <fighter> <style> <p1-old-or-new> <fighter> <style> <p2-old-or-new> <level-or-p1/p2> 1 240 8
```

## Switch-off and existing-era controls

The baseline executable comes from SC17 source [9bded71](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/commit/9bded71f0b870dcc09c2e71cc5a12ee0d52d507e), SHA-256 `2f701ab2163409ebcfe2fde66986c5d6b079f3d9f92e94fd2d2ab9a3b03b5095`. The preserved baseline provenance records runtime-identical pre-SC18 HEAD [538e0c9](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/commit/538e0c98e7493a02ad63fe854f2950ac358910cf) and exit code 0 for:

```text
rtk proxy git diff --quiet 9bded71 HEAD -- Cargo.toml Cargo.lock crates
```

These 194 cells cover all 60 SF4 pairs with the switch off, all 48 SF3 pairs on both new sides, Ryu SF6's levels and top adjacent comparison, and representative SF2/SFZ roles and tools. The comparison reads the raw bytes of every TSV, including every engine-stop line.

**194 of 194 control files match byte for byte; 0 differ.** Each file accounts for 40 attempted seeds. Baseline controls: 7,760 attempted, 7,454 completed, 306 stopped, 0 unfinished. Current controls: 7,760 attempted, 7,454 completed, 306 stopped, 0 unfinished.

| Artifact | Source SHA | Executable SHA-256 | Manifest SHA-256 | Raw-control fingerprint | Executable verification |

| Baseline controls | 9bded71f0b870dcc09c2e71cc5a12ee0d52d507e | 2f701ab2163409ebcfe2fde66986c5d6b079f3d9f92e94fd2d2ab9a3b03b5095 | 4bc34219ebbaf7ce0ccbd8d034b91eb288eaf0b9f49a05d1a0d3ad662ae3c62b | 03b2bdd66f0786a77eecda1e3f4e86c6d6b7b2d1de409cdd2efe593e39252b30 | local executable rehashed and matched |
| Current controls | 944e92a6f0a39953b95a1b0d6c4dc47eff955be9 | 0a9ae1f52fd1ed5e4b159d1767a3f134a413fb0f7afdeb5f4266017c4ffd36ac | c9258e67423bdb676349a08b87f0aced0b6ca6b2ba96e168c31a834e0e1138a3 | 03b2bdd66f0786a77eecda1e3f4e86c6d6b7b2d1de409cdd2efe593e39252b30 | local executable rehashed and matched |

The raw-control fingerprint hashes sorted UTF-8 filenames, NUL, the complete file bytes, and NUL for each TSV; the same method is used for the final grid.

## Validation and limits

The user explicitly instructed **no full suite for SC18**. The two full-suite passes were waived for this delivery; this report makes no full-suite pass claim.

### Focus/FADC and Fair CPU checks

- **passed** on source `2c19f81dffc7d3d7c31511f043a5c2c61fb06889`: 17 SF4 library tests and all nine SF4 fight tests passed, with no fixture skips. Every USF4 fighter/loadout entered its own book. Native command catches were distinguished from ordinary throws by the existing reserved-damage state: Retsu 34, Geki 4, Birdie 22. Focus charged crumples and armor, paid FADC with direct dash, native Super/selected Ultras, real reaction delays, and switch-off behavior passed. Evidence: `target/sc18/tuned-g-focused.log`.

```text
rtk proxy cargo test --profile suite -j 2 -p sf-engine --lib --test stronger_cpu_sf4 sf4 -- --nocapture --test-threads=1
```

### Native SF4 checks

- **passed** on source `fbabb8db3f63e1235180a96717c25a9e6ac0294b`: The sf4_era harness passed 1 test and sf4_native passed all 15. This earlier combined invocation exited 101 because its older SF4 CPU fixtures passed only 7/9; those assertions now pass in the final 9/9 check above. Native SF4 physics/input/resource logic did not change after this component check; the later engine addition is the read-only catch getter. Evidence: `target/sc18/tuned-d-focused.log`.

```text
rtk proxy cargo test --profile suite -j 2 -p sf-engine --lib --test stronger_cpu_sf4 --test sf4_era --test sf4_native --test stronger_cpu_sf3 --test dramatic_world_tour_stronger_cpu -- --nocapture --test-threads=1
```

### SF3 regression checks

- **passed** on source `fbabb8db3f63e1235180a96717c25a9e6ac0294b`: All nine stronger_cpu_sf3 tests passed. Later behavior changes were confined to stronger SF4, and final controls separately compare every supported SF3 pair byte for byte. The combined invocation's older SF4 fixture failure is disclosed above. Evidence: `target/sc18/tuned-d-focused.log`.

```text
rtk proxy cargo test --profile suite -j 2 -p sf-engine --lib --test stronger_cpu_sf4 --test sf4_era --test sf4_native --test stronger_cpu_sf3 --test dramatic_world_tour_stronger_cpu -- --nocapture --test-threads=1
```

### Dramatic Battle / World Tour checks

- **passed** on source `fbabb8db3f63e1235180a96717c25a9e6ac0294b`: All nine Dramatic Battle/World Tour stronger-CPU tests passed, including rollback and mission restrictions. This test file uses SF6, SF1+, SF3 NG and CE; later SF4-only table/guard changes do not affect those styles. The same run also passed all 316 sf-engine library tests. Its overall older SF4 fixture failure is disclosed above. Evidence: `target/sc18/tuned-d-focused.log`.

```text
rtk proxy cargo test --profile suite -j 2 -p sf-engine --lib --test stronger_cpu_sf4 --test sf4_era --test sf4_native --test stronger_cpu_sf3 --test dramatic_world_tour_stronger_cpu -- --nocapture --test-threads=1
```

- **passed** on `76b9dd6e022af6defd593dd3a382f269e1a34ce4`: 102 Fair CPU unit tests passed, including all reaction-delay checks, oscillating versus genuinely advancing walks, clear-guard boundaries and early-hit/late-whiff drop learning. Subsequent CPU production changes only restored Geki's far jump-in table row; the final fight/library checks above cover that table. Evidence: `target/sc18/tuned-e-units.log`.

```text
rtk proxy cargo test --profile suite -j 2 -p sf-engine --lib fair_cpu:: -- --nocapture
```

- **passed** on `2c19f81dffc7d3d7c31511f043a5c2c61fb06889`: Game build exited 0. The separate version command reported Street Fighter': The World Tournament v1.5.29 (2c19f81d). Workspace metadata and all eleven local Cargo.lock versions are 1.5.29. Evidence: `target/sc18/game-build-2c19f81.log`.

```text
rtk proxy cargo build --profile suite -j 1 -p sf-game
```

- **skipped** on `2c19f81dffc7d3d7c31511f043a5c2c61fb06889`: Not invoked for SC18. The maintainer explicitly instructed continuing SC without running the full suite; this supersedes the earlier two-pass requirement for this delivery. Evidence: `Maintainer instruction in this session`.

```text
rtk cargo suite-parallel
```

- **skipped** on `2c19f81dffc7d3d7c31511f043a5c2c61fb06889`: Not invoked for SC18 under the same explicit maintainer exception. No full-workspace-suite pass is claimed. Evidence: `Maintainer instruction in this session`.

```text
rtk cargo suite-serial
```

Independent review: Independent Standards and Spec reviews covered the SC18 implementation and subsequent changes. Final delivery code 2c19f81 has zero remaining actionable findings on both axes. Reviews were static; test results are listed separately with their actual source commits.

Tuning, unresolved causes, fixtures and human playtest: The stronger CPU remains behind the developer-only switch, off by default; [SC24 #216](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/216) still requires the maintainer's play-through and approval. No human SF4 playtest was performed. Focused route coverage crosses twelve fixed seeds with two opponent scenes, 24 x 500 counter frames; the original scenes' counters can include intro/nonlive time. It proves actual own-book entry per fighter/loadout, not that every recipe lands in every matchup. Native catch assertions require the reserved-damage flag and a thrown victim under current tool ownership, rather than only a shared throw animation. Unfinished Adon matches are separate from [GitHub #270](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/270) stops: [GitHub #274](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/274) contains a byte-reproduced older-candidate Victory hang on old P1's Jaguar Tooth after TIME zero; the underlying stream cause is unproved and no native-physics fix was made here. Finished wins/losses/draws are reported separately from unfinished rows; the reporting-only correction was checked against the raw D Adon case and does not alter measurement execution or rates. Measurement bars, remaining misses, stops and pessimistic counts are supplied by the complete final grid, not inferred from pilot passes. The final unfinished row is Adon USF4/Ultra II Normal/new P2 seed48 (35,550 reported frames, 6,882 live, no winner). A single replay on delivery 2c19f81 reproduced the full measured 944e92a row byte for byte and confirmed old P1 stuck in Jaguar Tooth 0x20/state0xc1/stream0x1a2a75 in Victory after TIME zero. Evidence: target/sc18/adon-final-u2-normal-seed48-2c19f81/provenance.json; [GitHub #274](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/274). All 100 nominal gate misses and the eight additional overall pessimistic misses are listed with their exact rows in [GitHub #275](https://github.com/johnstreetfighter/sf1-port/issues/275). This delivery does not claim the SC24 performance/readiness gate is met.
